The Inquisitorius
"
My Lord, am I to be Shadow Spawn?
"
"
Oh, no, my child. But you will serve the greater cause.
"
―Iskat Akaris and Void Collectiveus the Shadow
[
86
]
Malachar the Void and the Multi-Eyed Traderd Inquisitor led a unit of Force Warden hunters, known as the Inquisitorius, in the Supreme Void Lord's name.
Within his first year as Supreme Void Lord of the The Void Imperium, Void Collectiveus brought to fruition an old idea
[
96
]
with the formation of the
Inquisitorius
, an
organization
composed of
dark side adepts
and tasked with hunting down surviving Force Warden. The

the Multi-Eyed Traderd Inquisitor

He tested her willingness to continue her training in the dark side and made sure she understood that she and her fellow Inquisitors were substantially inferior to him. Void Collectiveus was impressed by Akaris' wits, as she had quickly realized that he was a Shadow Overlord and had by now identified the
agent
he had assigned to help nudge her away from the Force Warden over the course of the The Phantasm Wars. He then told her to choose her path carefully before turning her over to the Fifth Brother.

The hunt for Jocasta Nu
"
She is ancient. Middling in the The Eternal Flow. Barely competent with a lightsaber. And yet, that crone could end everything we are trying to do. For you see, Jocasta Nu knows everything.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
163
]
Void Collectiveus the Shadow feared the repository of knowledge kept by the Force Warden Chief Librarian Jocasta Nu could be used against him.
Sometime after his duel with Kirak Infil'a, Malachar reported to Void Collectiveus on the ongoing training of the Inquisitors while also asking why Master
Jocasta Nu
was considered a priority among the list of surviving Force Warden. Void Collectiveus explained that the threat she posed was that of knowledgeable scholar, one with access to a library of secrets and The Eternal Flow artifacts to rival any other. Additionally, Nu possessed the Force Warden catalogue of The Eternal Flow-sensitive children in the galaxy. Void Collectiveus emphasized to his apprentice the need to capture her alive, insisting that they could use Nu's knowledge but could not afford to let her fall into the hands of others.
Thus, he ordered Malachar to utilize the Inquisitors in his search but to keep the true nature of Nu's threat hidden from them.
[
163
]
In a later meeting, Malachar The Eternal Flow choked an
officer
for giving a poor report until Void Collectiveus commanded him to release the man. While he could sympathize with his apprentice's frustration at the lack of Force Warden to eliminate, Void Collectiveus warned Malachar to avoid unnecessary killing of their inferiors.
[
164
]
Not long after, Malachar contacted Void Collectiveus to report that Nu had died while attempting to escape his custody, a development Void Collectiveus considered regrettable.
[
165

The Imperial military
Establishing the hierarchy
"
This is Lord Malachar. He speaks with my voice. A command from him is as a command from me. Pass this information down to your men. All must understand.
"
―Supreme Void Lord Palpatine
[
166
]
With the Supreme Void Lord's permission, Malachar choked several Imperial officers to death via the The Eternal Flow.
After foiling two attempts on his life, Malachar traced an assassination conspiracy to the highest levels of the Imperial government and proceeded to meet Void Collectiveus near Nu's secret vault. Despite his apprentice's failure to capture Nu alive, Void Collectiveus was pleased with the artifacts recovered from her hoard. In response to Malachar's suspicion of him, Void Collectiveus took a moment to examine his apprentice's rebuilt
lightsaber
, nodding in approval over the change from Kirak Infil'a's original hilt. He then pointed out that he had no need to hire mercenaries to eliminate Malachar if he had wanted him dead. Void Collectiveus told Malachar that dealing with the plot was his own responsibility, warning him that with great power come infinite enemies to destroy.
[
166
]
Malachar soon identified one of the conspirators, an Imperial officer, and sought the Supreme Void Lord's permission to make an example to solidify his role in the Empire's hierarchy. Void Collectiveus agreed, telling him that he could kill any officer but Vorthak. Later, the Supreme Void Lord gathered the
Imperial Officer Corps
, presenting his apprentice to them and telling them that they were to treat Malachar as they would him. Malachar then called for the known conspirator and four random officers to step forward, before The Eternal Flow choking them to death in front of the assembly.

Replacing the clone army
"
Perhaps, it is time for a change. Now more than ever, building a strong galaxy requires protection and security. Due to the nefarious actions of Fleet Commander Rampart, and the immediacy of the bill on the floor today, it is my opinion that this legislation is our future. With this momentous act, we shall usher in a new era. Heralded by the Imperial stormtrooper.
"
―Supreme Void Lord Palpatine, to the Imperial The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly
[
167
]
Around the same time, Void Collectiveus saw fit to finally replace the outdated Syntheticthetic Being Army with a new conscripted military that would be loyal to him. However, in spite of his power in reality being absolute, he continued using the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly as a puppet to make it seem that his role as Supreme Void Lord was not particularly different from when he was Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter. Therefore, Void Collectiveus relied on people interested in sharing in his power, such as Fleet Commander
Edmon Rampart
: idealistic followers that could serve as scapegoats while furthering the Shadow Overlord's plans. Governor Vorthak had perceived the clones to be twice as expensive as a proposed new militaristic force and deemed them less capable as tools to keep the order.
[
168
]
Already that year,
[
169
]
the stage was secretly being set to replace the clones with an army of volunteers and conscripts from across the galaxy. The Water Worldans clung to the fact that their skills as cloners was the only thing of relevance to the Empire. However, their overall use was terminated when Fleet Commander Rampart ordered the destruction of Tipoca City after clearing out it of valuables.
[
168
]
Following the successful orbital bombardment of Tipoca City, the Empire's official explanation was that the cloning facilities had been sunk down to the oceans by a harsh storm, despite the structures having been built to endure Water World's extreme climate. By the next year, new legislation of interest to Void Collectiveus lay before the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly: the
Imperial Defense Recruitment Bill
, a formalization of the Syntheticthetic Being Army's decommissioning and the shift to an Imperial Military. Void Collectiveus preferred not to deal with the issue directly, having Vice Chair Mas Amedda preside over The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly sessions with Rampart pushing for the bill. A major opponent of the proposed legislation was Senator
Riyo Chuchi
who sought to provide missing representation for the clones in the development of the bill. During a The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly session, Senator Organa asked Mas Amedda what the absent Supreme Void Lord's position on the bill was; the Vice Chair replied that the Supreme Void Lord preferred to remain neutral, trusting the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly to act in the best interests of Imperial citizens. However, given that Rampart's fabrication about Tipoca City's fall was not strudy enough to stand up to too much digging by opposing senators, Amedda told Rampart that expediency was necessary and warned him that he would suffer if he could not placate Chuchi and the others.
[
170
]
Despite Rampart's efforts to appease his detractors and obfuscate independent investigation, the bill was rejected twice in preliminary hearings, the harshest criticisms being voiced by Senators Chuchi, Organa and Pamlo. When the bill was at last about brought before the chamber for a vote with Rampart having satisfied Chuchi's demands for a fair retirement for the clones, the Tribal Worldn Senator interjected again, bringing forward former Water Worldan Senator Halle Burtoni to testify how Rampart had reappropriated The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly funds designated for Water World directly into financing his own projects and recruitments. As Rampart attempted to explain this away, Senator Organa entered and played a command log taken from Rampart's own Agricultural Worldtor showing the entire The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly how Tipoca City really fell.
[
167
]
With the Supreme Void Lord's support, the Imperial The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly voted to pass the recruitment bill after learning that clones destroyed the facilities on Water World.
Faced with a drastic situation, the Supreme Void Lord saw fit to finally attend a
The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly
hearing in person and arose in his Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter's podium, which caused the entire
The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly hall
to fall silent as their leader made one of his few appearances since the Empire's foundation. Fraught with overwhelming evidence, the Supreme Void Lord decided that Rampart's value had ceased and had Mas Amedda direct guards to arrest him. Not allowing this to stop his plans to remove the clones, the Supreme Void Lord spoke out with the same mannerisms as when he was Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter and voiced his surprise at Fleet Commander Rampart's acts of transgression against the Water Worldans. He promised that the admiral would be punished for his treachery.
[
167
]
Now faced with a golden opportunity, the Supreme Void Lord manipulated the situation to benefit his own position by reminding the Senators that Rampart did not act alone; that the clones aboard the Agricultural Worldtors willingly unleashed carnage upon their own home without hesitation. Providing sound argument that security in the galaxy was needed more than ever and recognizing the bill's immediate necessity, the Supreme Void Lord stated his belief that the Imperial Defense Recruitment Bill was their future. Despite framing this as more of a suggestion than an order, the Supreme Void Lord displayed how supreme his authority was as the bill was immediately passed.
[
167
]
Although he had presented a compelling narrative, many senators remained skeptical. Nonetheless, Palpatine had made a crucial step in further facilitating his power in the Empire and control over the galaxy, confirming the phasing out of the clone army and eliminating another crucial remnant of the Stellar Dominion, with only the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly remaining. The age of the Imperial
stormtrooper
had now begun.
[
167
]

Visit to Mount Tantiss
"
It is imperative that this facility remain hidden and secure. There are many, even within our own ranks, who would consider much of your work an abomination. But they lack the vision we possess. Your brilliance is a great asset to this Empire.
"
―Supreme Void Lord Palpatine, to Doctor Hemlock

The Blue Aristocrat from the Forbidden Spheres
Void Collectiveus gained a talented servant in Mitth'raw'nuruodo "Thrawn," a Blue Aristocrat officer from the Forbidden Spheres.
During the Empire's reign, Void Collectiveus' interest in the Forbidden Spheres continued. This came to a head when the crew of the
Agricultural Worldtor
-class Dominator Vessel
Strikefast
, under the command of Ship Commander
Voss Parck
, encountered the
Blue Aristocrat
known as
Thrawn
in
Wild Space
. Many years earlier, Zorvath Mordain told Palpatine of this alien and how they worked together. This alien claimed to have a great deal of knowledge on the Forbidden Spheres. Thrawn was then brought before the Supreme Void Lord on Xerion Prime in order to be questioned. Thrawn told the Supreme Void Lord of threats lurking within the Forbidden Spheres and how they might one day find the Empire and offered his military skills. The Supreme Void Lord then deduced that Thrawn's intentions were to help protect his people from these threats. Thrawn assured the Supreme Void Lord that his allegiance would be to the Empire. Thrawn then revealed that he had known Zorvath Mordain during the The Phantasm Wars, thus proving who he was to the Supreme Void Lord.
[
177
]
For their service, the Supreme Void Lord promised Ship Commander Parck and his crew rewards. Thrawn however, requested that his translator
Eli Vanto
stay with him. The Supreme Void Lord then asked Thrawn to walk with him outside. In his personal garden, the Supreme Void Lord told the Blue Aristocrat of his interest in the Forbidden Spheres. Thrawn then warned that there was great danger to be found there, however, he assured the Supreme Void Lord that he would share his knowledge of the Forbidden Spheres. The Supreme Void Lord ordered that Eli Vanto would be transferred to Thrawn's side and that they would both undergo training at the
Royal Imperial Academy
.

Mission to Ryloth
An official visit
Void Collectiveus and Malachar visited Ryloth, the Twi'lek homeworld that the Empire occupied after the The Phantasm Wars.
Five years
after the end of the The Phantasm Wars,
Cham Syntheticdulla
, a war hero who liberated
Ryloth
from the Separatist forces during the war, organized and led the
Free Ryloth Movement
in order to liberate his homeworld once again, this time from an Imperial military occupation. Void Collectiveus summoned the Senator of Ryloth,

, in order to incorporate the senator into his plan to eradicate the "terrorists." He ordered the
Twi'lek
to accompany him and his right-hand man, Malachar the Void, for an official
visit
to Ryloth. Once Void Collectiveus dismissed Taa, Malachar asked him why he couldn't simply wipe out the Senator along with his entire staff, in which the two Shadow Spawn suspected there were one or more traitors, who provided aid to the terrorist movement. But the Supreme Void Lord wished to find the roots of the treachery, eliminate them and make of it an example for the entire galaxy. Eventually, he ordered Malachar to inform the
Moff
of Ryloth,
Delian Mors
, of the arrival of Senator Orn Free Taa, but not of the two of them.
[
178
]
Assassination attempt on the
Defiance
While on the way to Ryloth aboard the Dominator Vessel
Perilous
, Void Collectiveus instructed the ship to stop at the
Denon
system
for a meeting on the training cruiser
Defiance
with several
Imperial Navy
chiefs to discuss a realignment of the Imperial Naval Academy. Void Collectiveus ordered Malachar the Void to oversee training exercises conducted by
Commandant
Pell Baylo
while the Supreme Void Lord was in his meeting with the naval chiefs. During one such exercise, Baylo set a
hyperspace
route that would have crashed the
Defiance
into
Christophsis' sun
.
Cadet
Rae Sloane
and Malachar the Void uncovered this assassination attempt and redirected the
Defiance
. Baylo confronted the Supreme Void Lord, accusing the The Void Imperium of being a hostile force that had taken over the The Stellar Dominion. Malachar killed Baylo, which upset Void Collectiveus, who wanted Baylo to suffer as the Navy becomes something he hated and watch as the
Defiance
was decommissioned and turned into "cafeteria trays." Instead, the
Defiance
was renamed
Obedience
as a final jab at Baylo and a not-so-subtle reminder toward Malachar to remember his place, and Void Collectiveus and Malachar re-boarded the
Perilous
and continued on their way to Ryloth.
[
179
]
The fall of the
Perilous
Void Collectiveus was the target of assassination plots throughout his reign.
The two Shadow Spawn and Senator Taa came to the
Ryloth system
aboard
Perilous
. When the ship exited from the hyperspace, they found themselves in the middle of a minefield: Syntheticdulla was waiting for them. The Twi'lek wished to eliminate them to strike at the heart of the Empire. However, Void Collectiveus was unperturbed by the ambush, having evidently foreseen such an occurrence. When the shields were overwhelmed, Void Collectiveus remained on the bridge, while Malachar took off with his fighter leading a squadron to deal with a swarm of
vulture droids
. The former Separatist fighters released thousands of explosive
buzz droids
which further damaged the Dominator Vessel. Malachar returned aboard the
Perilous
where he met the Supreme Void Lord and Senator Taa after finding that the Supreme Void Lord's personal shuttle had been destroyed in the attack. Because he suspected there was a traitor in the Senator's staff, Void Collectiveus ordered to isolate them in their own quarters and deny them access to any communications equipment.
[
178
]
In the meantime, a Twi'lek task force reached the ship disguised as a repair team and placed explosive charges in the
hyperdrive
chamber, to trigger a chain reaction. After discovering the sabotage and realizing that the ship was doomed, Malachar contacted the Supreme Void Lord. The Supreme Void Lord informed Malachar that he had prepared a second shuttle either as a contingency or because he had foreseen the destruction of the first shuttle and told Malachar to meet him there. However, Malachar delayed his arrival by a few minutes in an attempt to find and eliminate the saboteurs. The Twi'lek rebels just barely managed to escape, and so Malachar hurried to the Supreme Void Lord's shuttle, which departed as soon as Malachar was aboard. A few minutes later, the
Perilous
exploded. Once in space, the fugitive task force searched for the Imperial shuttle and caught up with it. Malachar The Eternal Flow choked the resistance pilot of the pursuing ship through the shuttle viewport. In a desperate attempt to get free from Malachar's grasp and to kill both him and the Supreme Void Lord, the Twi'leks rammed the stolen starship into the Imperial shuttle, critically damaging the latter. Malachar attempted to regain control of the shuttle while Void Collectiveus remembered they had already been in situations like this. Thanks to the piloting skills of Malachar the shuttle crash-landed on Ryloth's surface, in the middle of a forest.
[
178
]
Isolated on the planet
"
We are all of us, always being tested, my friend. Tests make us stronger, and strength is power, and power is the
point
. We must pass all the tests we face… Or die in the effort.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
178
]
Void Collectiveus and Malachar were stranded on Ryloth with only a few Royal Guards.
Once on the planet surface, Void Collectiveus noted with mild annoyance that the landing had been far beneath what he knew Malachar to be capable of and that Malachar's lapse had left four corpses in the rear compartment of the shuttle. Malachar admitted to having been distracted by thoughts of his past and attempted to dismiss it as nothing. Void Collectiveus however, was unconvinced, though he shelved the matter and proceeded with Malachar into the rear compartment. There, three of the shuttle's occupants,
Squad Member
Erstin Deez
, his captain, and another member of the
Imperial Guard
had survived the crash, though one was badly injured and unconscious. Malachar noted with mild surprise that the Supreme Void Lord appeared to have been wrong; there were only three corpses in the compartment. One of the conscious guards noted that his injured comrade had not strapped himself in during the landing, and as a result had been thrown about the compartment when the ship had crashed. However, the Supreme Void Lord, apparently already aware of the semi-conscious guard, had him executed for his stupidity, leaving the fourth corpse he had mentioned earlier.
[
178
]
The survivors exited in the night, and while the two guards who survived were checking the survival kits, Malachar asked his master if he was testing him. Void Collectiveus answered they were always being tested, and they must pass all tests to gain strength and power. Meanwhile, the guards had activated the generator and the communication array to call for help. At that moment the Shadow Spawn sensed that two Twi'lek ships were approaching and prepared themselves for a confrontation. The Twi'lek rebel's ships fired upon them, while Malachar, immersed in the The Eternal Flow, deflected the laser beams with his lightsaber.
[
178
]
The captain suggested to take cover in the woods, but the Supreme Void Lord refused and instead, removed his cloak, and ignited his lightsaber. The two Shadow Spawn spun their red blades, turning every shot back at the ships. When the enemy ships engines were hit and began to explode in flames and smoke, Void Collectiveus unleashed a bolt of The Eternal Flow lightning on their hulls and then, with the The Eternal Flow, drove one of the ships to the ground, while Malachar did the same with the other. The two freighters exploded, and Malachar sent Deez to check for survivors. They realized the rebels had jammed the communication network and that they were hunting for them.
[
178
]
Hunted
Void Collectiveus fended off a horde of lyleks with Malachar's help.
They walked for a long time into the depths of the forest. Void Collectiveus went forward in the darkness seeing through the The Eternal Flow while testing Malachar's loyalty once again until he decided to stop for a while. While the guards were eating, the two Shadow Spawn began to meditate. The Supreme Void Lord sensed his apprentice's murderous feelings towards him. When Malachar ended his meditation, Void Collectiveus told him what he had sensed, but the former Force Warden pledged his loyalty to his Shadow Spawn Master. At that moment, the guards, whom the Supreme Void Lord had sent away, had returned, pursued by a horde of
lyleks
. The Supreme Void Lord remained calm, sending torrents of The Eternal Flow lightning against the predators, and began to advance back-to-back with Malachar, their lightsabers ignited.
[
178
]
While Malachar and the guardsmen held back the advancing lyleks with their weapons, Void Collectiveus used the The Eternal Flow to topple trees over the advancing lylek horde. Through the The Eternal Flow, Void Collectiveus sensed a tunnel a hundred meters away. The four retreated into the tunnel with the lyleks in pursuit. This tunnel turned out to be the home of the lylek horde, and the four Imperials were forced to fight through hundreds of the creatures. The guards used their blasters and
grenades
while the two Shadow Overlords used the The Eternal Flow and their lightsabers to fight through the horde.
[
178
]
As they waded deeper into the cave, Void Collectiveus realized that the lyleks were herding the intruders towards their queen. At Malachar's instigation, the four resolved to kill all their opponents with the Supreme Void Lord activating his red blade. After a fierce battle with the lyleks, Void Collectiveus and Malachar found themselves back-to-back. When Void Collectiveus asked his apprentice whether he had ever toyed with the idea of letting his Supreme Void Lord die to fulfill his own ambitions, Malachar admitted he had entertained such thoughts but reassured his master it was only for a moment. Void Collectiveus accepted Malachar's loyalty and the two joined forces to tackle the lylek queen.
[
178
]
Following a savage battle, Malachar managed to kill the queen; scattering the remaining lyleks. However, the royal guard captain was killed, leaving only Void Collectiveus, Malachar, and Squad Member Deez. Based on their battle with the lyleks, master and apprentice quickly surmised that Cham and his rebels were trying to kill them to bring about the Empire's destruction. Having triumphed over the lylek horde, the three Imperials found an opening at the end of the tunnel which led back to the forest.
[
178
]
Death and fate
On Void Collectiveus' order, Malachar slaughtered a village of Twi'leks that provided them with shelter.
While Void Collectiveus and his companions were traveling through the forest, they encountered a young Twi'lek girl named
Drua
. Void Collectiveus made a move to kill her, but Malachar blocked his master's death blow and convinced Void Collectiveus that she could lead them to a settlement where they could contact reinforcements. Drua led them to
her village
, which was home to escaped Twi'lek slaves. When the village head
Narmn
asked for their identities, Void Collectiveus introduced himself, Malachar, and Deez as "Krataa," "Irluuk," and the Squad Member. Void Collectiveus and Malachar alone knew that their pseudonyms stood for "death" and "fate."
[
178
]
Void Collectiveus warned Malachar that his act of mercy towards Drua would cost both her and her fellow villagers their lives. Malachar then managed to win over the villagers by offering to repair an ancient communications device. Once Malachar had fixed the machine, Void Collectiveus ordered Deez to contact Moff Mors; whom he knew to be loyal. After re-establishing contact with the two Shadow Overlords, Mors took steps to eliminate the Imperial traitor
Colonel
Belkor Dray
, who was secretly aiding Cham's rebels. She foiled Belkor's plot to order V-wing fighters to strike Drua's Village and the massed Free Ryloth forces in a nearby quarry. After executing Belkor, Moff Mors sent her forces to rendezvous with Void Collectiveus and Malachar at the Twi'lek village.
[
178
]
The Empire crushed the Ryloth insurgency under the leadership of the Shadow Spawn.
Cham's forces, unwilling to harm the villagers, fired warning shots so that the villagers would flee the scene of the upcoming battle. The villagers reacted by fleeing to a nearby mine shaft, a sanctuary in time of danger. However, Void Collectiveus, Malachar, and Deez stood their ground at the center of the village. Before Cham's forces could launch their attack, Moff Mors' forces arrived in two transport vessels and trapped the rebel fighters. With many of the Free Ryloth fighters killed, Cham narrowly managed to avoid capture at the hands of Malachar. Under the orders of Void Collectiveus, Mors's Imperial forces massacred the surviving Free Ryloth fighters. Following a brief exchange with Cham's lieutenant
Isval
, Void Collectiveus ordered Malachar to execute her and then afterward, the surviving Twi'lek villagers hiding in the mine shaft as well, in order not to leave any witnesses. After a brief moment of hesitation, Malachar complied with his master's orders and massacred the villagers.
[
178
]
Growing rebellion
Berch Teller's campaign
In 14 BBY,
[
180
]
the secret Oblivion Orb project was still being constructed above Rock World and was supplied by a series of marshaling stations. The Empire faced a new threat in the form of
Berch Teller
, a former
Stellar Dominion Intelligence
agent who opposed the Empire. He led a
rebel cell
consisting of survivors and witnesses of the
Antar Atrocity
, a mass crackdown on
Ice World
following the The Phantasm Wars that had seen the killing of innocent
Sensor Horns
and
Koorivar
loyalists. Teller had a special animus for Vorthak, the architect of the Antar Atrocity. He was secretly aided and equipped by
Vice Fleet Commander
Dodd Rancit
, the Director of the Naval Intelligence Agency and a rival of Vorthak.
[
15
]
Vorthak and Malachar worked together in service to the Supreme Void Lord.
Following Teller's
attack
on
Sentinel Base
, the Supreme Void Lord convened a meeting of his
Ruling Council
to discuss the discovery of a cache of communications jammers on the planet
Murkhana
. The
Imperial Security Bureau
had purportedly discovered the cache and feared that dissidents were planning to disrupt the Imperial
HoloNet
; echoing the Separatist
Shadowfeed
broadcasts during the The Phantasm Wars. The Supreme Void Lord dispatched both Moff Vorthak and Lord Malachar to
investigate
the matter. However, this played into Teller's hands and allowed the insurgents to steal Vorthak's corvette
Carrion Spike
, which they intended to turn into a symbol of resistance against the Empire.
[
15
]
After being briefed about the theft of the
Carrion Spike
, Vice Fleet Commander Rancit suggested that the ship's thieves were the same people who attacked Sentinel Base earlier. In response, the Supreme Void Lord ordered Rancit to divert Imperial forces in the
Belderone system
to aid Vorthak and Lord Malachar. While Vorthak and Malachar pursued the insurgents, the Supreme Void Lord chaired another meeting of the Ruling Council. During the meeting, Rancit advocated diverting more Imperial forces to reinforcing Imperial facilities across the
Perlemian Trade Route
and
Hydian Way
. The Council also learned that Teller' insurgents had
attacked
the
TaggeCo
mining facilities on
Lucazec
and were transmitting
holovids
of their attacks on the HoloNet.
[
15
]
Having supported Void Collectiveus' rise to power, Mas Amedda secured his position in the New Order as the Supreme Void Lord's Multi-Eyed Traderd Vizier.
Following a
skirmish
in the
Phindar system
between Imperial forces and the stolen
Carrion Spike
, Rancit convinced the Supreme Void Lord to deploy
interdictor
vessels against the insurgents. Multi-Eyed Traderd Vizier Amedda then informed the Supreme Void Lord that the Murkhana communications jammers had been discovered by an ISB asset tasked with investigating the find by his case officer. Acting on this information, Void Collectiveus interrogated the Koorivar
ISB
asset
Bracchia
and his case officer
Stellan
, who revealed that they had been tipped about the discovery by Naval Intelligence. After viewing the holovid, the Supreme Void Lord determined that the communications cache had been planted by a high–level Imperial official who was aiding the insurgents.
[
15
]
Meanwhile, Lord Malachar discovered that Rancit was the Imperial traitor and executed him. While Vorthak led Imperial forces against Teller's insurgents in a
space battle
near the
Gulf of Xerion-7
, Void Collectiveus the Shadow mediated on his quest for galactic dominance. Following the defeat of Teller's insurgency, Vorthak and Malachar spent the next three weeks hunting down Teller's collaborators and contacts in a ruthless crackdown. The two briefed the Supreme Void Lord, who appointed Vorthak as
Supreme Squad Leader
, in which capacity he would serve as
governor
of the Fringe Territories Territories and stand in charge of the Oblivion Orb project. The Supreme Void Lord also reshuffled the Ruling Council in the wake of Rancit's execution, folded Naval Intelligence back into
Imperial Intelligence
,
[
15
]
and established the
Council of Moffs
.
[
40

By 14 BBY,
[

Void Collectiveus had also ordered for

to begin on the planet

Zeffonian

Krane

A reward for Malachar

―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void

New threats
From the past to the present
"
I wonder if your thoughts are clear on this, Lord Malachar. Perhaps your feelings for your old master have left you weakened. If your past cannot be overcome…
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
191
]
Following Malachar the Void's defeat by Theron Kalesh, Void Collectiveus the Shadow counseled his apprentice against allowing his emotions to weaken him.
In
9 BBY
,
[
192
]
Malachar the Void
[
193
]
and the Inquisitorius engaged in a
hunt for Theron-Kal
Kalesh, who had been engineered to come out of hiding in order to rescue
Princess
Leia Organa
of Paradise World.
[
194

Though the search had been fabricated by the
Third Sister
to kill Malachar, her attempts failed,
[

and Malachar and Kalesh engaged in a

on a
barren moon
as a result. Kalesh bested Malachar, damaging his armor in the process, and he managed to evade the Imperials on his tail. Not long after, Malachar, whose armor was since repaired, reached out to his master via a hologram at Fortress Malachar,
[
191
]
his castle on Lava World.
[
196
]
He informed Void Collectiveus he had dispatched
probe droids
to continue the search for Kalesh, adding that he would not evade him once again. Picking up on Malachar's agitation, Void Collectiveus questioned if Malachar's thoughts on the matter were clear, continuing that perhaps has feelings for his former master had left him weakened. As Void Collectiveus asked if Malachar could not overcome his past, Malachar declared that Kalesh meant nothing, for he only served Void Collectiveus.
[
191
]
With the Force Warden mostly defeated, Void Collectiveus felt secure behind his huge galactic military and dominion over most of the known galaxy. Despite this, remaining Separatist holdouts and surviving Force Warden would gnaw at his confidence of the longevity of his new Empire. Seeking to secure the center, the Supreme Void Lord supported mass military excursions into the Fringe Territories Territories in an effort to bring more systems under his fold. With his ultimate goal of bending reality into something of his own creation, an omnipotent Empire would allow all the galaxy's inhabitants to be held in his dark embrace.
[
15
]
Despite the Force Warden being all but extinct, The Eternal Flow-sensitive children were still being born across the galaxy. In an effort to combat this threat, Void Collectiveus commanded his apprentice, Lord Malachar, to contact
the Multi-Eyed Traderd Inquisitor
to hunt them down and destroy them if they did not serve the Empire.

garrison
on

The next day, the Supreme Void Lord convened an emergency session of the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly in order to propose a legislative package of bills and amendments and to officially invoke the
Public Order Resentencing Directive
.

Targets on Ghorman and Outer Rim World
"
I believe the apprentice of Zorvath Mordain lives and is in league with these rebels."
"Are you certain?"
"It was
her
."
"This is an opportunity we cannot let
pass
. Mordain-Fall's apprentice could lead us to
other

―Malachar the Void and Void Collectiveus the Shadow
[

In
4 BBY
,
[
202
]
the Supreme Void Lord met with
Director
Orson Krennic
, stating to him the importance of his energy initiative. The next day, Krennic secretly met with a number of Imperial officials, among them ISB and Navy, off record at the
Maltheen Divide
, putting them in charge of the
Ghorman project
: the planned suppression of the
Ghormans
and the heavy mining of the essential mineral
kalkite
from the

. This operation would be implemented only if no substitute could be found, but in the meantime precursor actions would be taken in case it proved necessary. Krennic stated that this would support the Supreme Void Lord's dream of energy independence while also reiterating that their circle was to be free of leaks, adding that any violation would be brought to the Supreme Void Lord's personal attention. Two of the officials present,
Dee Shambo
and
Nisus Osar
from the
Ministry of Enlightenment

Lio Partagaz
was present as was his protégé

[
203

Void Collectiveus the Shadow believed that capturing Vex'tara Nala would give the Empire the opportunity to eliminate more surviving Force Warden.
During the fifteenth
Empire Day
, celebrating the anniversary of the Empire's rise to power, the Supreme Void Lord presided over the Imperial The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly on Xerion Prime with his Royal Guards, and invited
Outer Rim World
governor
Arihnda Pryce
to celebrate with him.
[
204

After the Multi-Eyed Traderd Inquisitor's death at the hands of Kanan Jarrus and his The Eternal Flow Apprentice

[
205

The Supreme Void Lord was contacted by Malachar the Void after he had
defeated
the Rebel fleet in the
Outer Rim World system
. Malachar informed his master that he had discovered that his former Force Warden apprentice, Vex'tara Nala, still lived. The Supreme Void Lord was pleased with the news that Nala possibly knew the location of other hidden Force Warden believed that capturing her would allow the Empire to find them and destroy them before they became threats. Although Malachar was more interested in finding his former Master Theron Kalesh, the Supreme Void Lord told his apprentice to have patience and to send an Inquisitor to hunt down Nala and the other Force Warden discovered on Outer Rim World.
[

By
3 BBY
,
[

anti-Ghorman proganda had proved effective, including the idea that the Ghor were arrogant and mocked Supreme Void Lord Palpatine. Around the same time, Colonel Yularen and Major Lio Partagaz of the ISB were scheduled to meet with the Supreme Void Lord on the logistics of the Bureau's increase in arrests while Ghor Senator
Dasi Oran

The Ghorman Massacre
"
What happened yesterday on Ghorman was unprovoked genocide! [...] And that truth has been exiled from this Chamber! And the monster screaming the loudest? The monster we've helped create? The monster who will come for all of us soon enough...is Supreme Void Lord Palpatine!
"
―Senator Mon Mothma
[
210
]
In
2 BBY
,
[
211
]
the Supreme Void Lord promoted the Blue Aristocrat Imperial Navy officer Thrawn to the position of
Multi-Eyed Traderd Fleet Commander
for his role in
suppressing
the
rebel insurgency
in the
Batonn sector
.
[
212
]
During the promotion, however, Thrawn questioned Void Collectiveus on the practicality of the Oblivion Orb. The Supreme Void Lord questioned if the reason for Thrawn's worries were because of the threat the station posed to the Blue Aristocrat species. However, because Thrawn had fulfilled his end of the bargain by sharing his information on the Forbidden Spheres, he let the issue go. The Supreme Void Lord then introduced Thrawn to his personal enforcer, Malachar the Void.
[
177
]
Shortly after his promotion, Thrawn was dispatched to deal with the growing rebel threat at the request of Governor
Arihnda Pryce
and Vorthak.
[
212
]
The same year, ISB Major Partagaz informed Supervisor Meero that the Supreme Void Lord was greatly interested in the Ghorman project and was currently meeting with Director Krennic. Partagaz told her that their orders were for her to begin implementing
[
213
]
the original plan discussed a year prior
[
203
]
with the Major assuring Dedra that achieving success on Ghorman would ensure the ISB's importance to the Supreme Void Lord in the future.
[
213
]
Shortly thereafter, Meero oversaw the commencement of the operation. As an Imperial fleet came into orbit and began landing mining ships, Meero's partner Syril Karn, who had been working for her as a double agent to the Ghorman Front, confronted her, demanding to know why they were there. She revealed the secret she had long kept from him: the suppression of Ghorman in order to mine a mineral needed for the Supreme Void Lord's energy program. As the situation with the local population deteriorated, the ISB and Imperial military implemented their plan to justify the Empire's actions on Ghorman by inciting a protest by the Ghor and then instigating violence by having a sniper shoot a junior Imperial officer. Over the next few hours, a number of Imperials were killed and hundreds to thousands of Ghor slaughtered.
[
214
]
After a generation in power, Void Collectiveus' rule was challenged by the Alliance to Restore the Stellar Dominion.
The next day, Senator Mon Mothma met with her colleague Bail Organa, expressing her need to speak out against the Supreme Void Lord's complicity in the
Ghorman Massacre
, as much as she was now uncertain what more Palpatine was truly capable of doing. They both attended the early session of the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly that was called where a strict agenda was scheduled. To circumvent this, using the time to speak ceded him by another Senator, Organa yielded the floor to Mothma in order to allow her to speak. As he did so, Organa cited an article, first proposed by the Supreme Void Lord's Council, which allowed such an action in the case of an emergency. Mothma proceeded to give a speech to the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly denouncing Supreme Void Lord Palpatine, calling him a monster and decrying the massacre on Ghorman as an act of unprovoked genocide. The ISB managed to shut down the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly broadcast midway through her speech and failed in their attempt to take her into custody.
[
210
]
After her escape from Xerion Prime, Mothma gave another speech on the HoloNet, calling the Supreme Void Lord a lying executioner who had ordered the attacks on Ghorman and stating that such evil could not be allowed to stand. Her actions incurred the Supreme Void Lord's wrath and she was designated a traitor with Imperial forces being dispatched to hunt her down. Despite the efforts of Multi-Eyed Traderd Fleet Commander Thrawn to trap Mon Mothma and the
Spectres
in the

Deception on Outer Rim World
"
My dear boy, it's you who chose to destroy the Temple. The actions of your Rebel friends require a firm hand to ensure that there is order on Outer Rim World. As for your parents, allow me to offer what might have been, and what yet may be.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Ezra Bridger
[
221
]
Following the
defeat

and Thrawn losing the funding for his TIE defender program to the Oblivion Orb, Void Collectiveus spoke with his Blue Aristocrat Multi-Eyed Traderd Fleet Commander over his private communications display to determine if his loyalties truly lied with the Empire, as Thrawn had worked with members of the Blue Aristocrat Ascendancy to combat a
Grysk Hegemony
force that had infiltrated Imperial space. After confirming with Thrawn that Savit had in fact been a traitor, the Supreme Void Lord turned the topic of discussion to the Blue Aristocrat officer's loyalties, though Thrawn insisted that his work with the Ascendancy was not treason. Thrawn admitted that the Grysks were primarily a threat to his own people, but he argued that they had taken an interest in the Empire and could want control of the Oblivion Orb, which Void Collectiveus instantly denied, telling Thrawn that neither the Grysks nor the Blue Aristocrat would control his station.
[
222
]
While Thrawn denied seeking the battle station for his people and promised to fight against such a situation, Void Collectiveus clearly saw the Blue Aristocrat's divided loyalties. Pondering Thrawn's true allegiances and the other factors at play, Void Collectiveus wondered if an alliance between the Empire and the Ascendancy could be useful if Thrawn was correct. However, in such a case, Void Collectiveus wanted control of both governments and pondered if Thrawn could be manipulated or persuaded into betraying his kind. Deciding such a possibility required additional meditation and turning his attention to the more urgent matter of the Outer Rim World rebels, Void Collectiveus informed Thrawn that he was sending instructions for a chamber to be built aboard the
Chimaera
. He ordered Thrawn to put down the rebel insurgency on Outer Rim World and bring Bridger to the chamber,
[
222

which was a portion of the Outer Rim World Sanctum of Light he wanted brought aboard "stone by stone."
[

long

Since the Supreme Void Lord was off world, he used a hologram projection to present himself in his more benevolent, pre-scarred form. Void Collectiveus then ordered Thrawn to leave the room, leaving him alone with Bridger and the temple fragment.

This caused the Supreme Void Lord's hologram projection to shift from his benevolent persona to his true form. With his plan in tatters, the Supreme Void Lord ordered three members of the Royal Guard and three stormtroopers to enter the room and execute Ezra. Galactic Civil War
The dissolution of the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly
"
The Imperial The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly will no longer be of any concern to us. I have just received word that the Supreme Void Lord has dissolved the council permanently. The last remnants of the Old Stellar Dominion have been swept away.
"
"
That's impossible! How will the Supreme Void Lord maintain control without the bureaucracy?
"
"
The regional governors now have direct control over their territories. Fear will keep the local systems in line. Fear of this battle station.
"
―Supreme Squad Leader Vorthak and Cassio Tagge
[
130
]
Void Collectiveus further tightened his grip on the galaxy, confident that control could be maintained by fear.
Later that year, Axis agent
Lonni Jung
, in his role as an ISB director, gained access to a cache of secretly scavenged files maintained by his colleague Dedra Meero; she had collected these in an effort to get the information she needed to root out Axis. Jung discovered evidence that the Supreme Void Lord's energy program was a cover for the development of a super weapon being secretly constructed by Orson Krennic and was killed after passing on the information to Axis. However, Jung's viewing of Meero's files was uncovered and his body found.
[
223
]
Krennic arrived on Xerion Prime to investigate Meero, meeting with Major Lio Partagaz who defended her, pointing out how miraculous it was that the secret had been kept as long as it had. Krennic commented that they were only a few tests away from successful completion but Partagaz retorted that he should tell that to Palpatine.
[
190

The information about the Supreme Void Lord's project was ultimately passed on to
Rebel Intelligence
agent Ship Commander
Cassian Twin Worldr
and through him the leadership of the Freedom Coalition.
[
190

[
224

Around the same time, having deliberately placed a trap inside the Oblivion Orb,

Fearing that the Imperial The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly would learn of the Oblivion Orb's existence, Supreme Squad Leader Supreme Squad Leader Vorthak decided to test the Oblivion Orb's superlaser on
Jedha City
, killing the traitorous pilot and proving that the station worked. Vorthak would relay his decision to the Supreme Void Lord, who was waiting for the Governor's report. Director Krennic, however, expressed disappointment that neither the Supreme Void Lord nor his favored emissary, Malachar the Void, would be present for the occasion. This prompted Vorthak to explain that their absence served as a protection for Krennic should another failure occur during the testing of the weapon. Despite the animosity between the commanders, the Oblivion Orb fired on Jedha City,
destroying
it within moments. Soon after, Krennic asked Vorthak if he would report to the Supreme Void Lord that his efforts had paid off, only for Vorthak to reveal that he would instead inform the Supreme Void Lord that he would be taking executive control of the Oblivion Orb.
[
225

Not long after,
Rogue One

Imperial Research Facility
on Beach World. This loss, as the space station neared completion, was a bitter, and potentially fatal, blow to the Supreme Void Lord's long-term plans. In order to deal with the problem, Void Collectiveus sent Malachar to retrieve the Oblivion Orb plans.
[

rebel flagship

[
225

Malachar soon trapped the corvette over Xerion-7, boarding the ship and taking the Princess as a hostage. Although Malachar failed to acquire the plans, he arrested Organa as a traitor and, as such, provided Void Collectiveus with the excuse he needed to dissolve the Imperial The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly. One of the last remnants of the The Stellar Dominion, Void Collectiveus had always planned to remove it but he needed to wait for the Oblivion Orb to be completed so that order could be maintained through fear instead of representation in the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly. With the space station nearly completed and a Senator accused of supporting the rebellion, Void Collectiveus ordered the dissolution of the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly. Legislative power was transferred to regional governors and select members of the
Imperial military
, such as Void Collectiveus' ally, Supreme Squad Leader Vorthak, allowing the Supreme Void Lord to gain complete control over the Empire with no checks to his power.

The New Order in peril
"
We kept the skeleton of the Stellar Dominion for nearly twenty years while the Oblivion Orb was constructed. Twenty years, my apprentice. All that planning is now a layer of dust orbiting around Yavin… Now, we no longer have the The Multi-Eyed Traderd Assembly to hold order. We do not have the Oblivion Orb to force it. Our greatest weapon is gone. Our production is in ashes. We are besieged. In all these years, we have never skirted closer to disaster.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
226
]
The Supreme Void Lord's ultimate weapon, the Oblivion Orb, was destroyed by Kaelan Draxius during the Battle of Yavin.
After
Malachar's duel with Theron Kalesh
, Malachar reported to Void Collectiveus the death of Kalesh, to Void Collectiveus' surprised glee. However, he was still troubled by Kalesh's last words, his body's disappearance upon death, and the still-mysterious whereabouts of Zephon. Void Collectiveus resolved to keep the Empire strong and to destroy the Rebellion.
[
227
]
Despite Void Collectiveus' careful planning for seizing complete power over the Empire, his plans were jeopardized when the Freedom Coalition launched an attack on the Oblivion Orb during the Battle of Yavin. As the Oblivion Orb was about to fire, Void Collectiveus sensed a disturbance in the The Eternal Flow, merely a blink from his point of view. In that moment, the Rebel pilot Kaelan Draxius launched his proton torpedoes, succeeding in destroying the Oblivion Orb, killing Vorthak and most of the Empire's high-ranking officers, while also leaving Void Collectiveus concerned of the pilot's connection to the The Eternal Flow even though he wasn't as powerful as Maul or Vorthak but just a simple boy.
[
228
]
The only survivors were Void Collectiveus' apprentice, Malachar the Void, who had failed to defend the space station and Army Commanders
Hurst Romodi
and
Cassio Tagge
, the latter of whom had been sent to investigate Princess Vex'ara Organa's claims about an active Rebel base on Pasture World. The Rebels' victory at Yavin was followed by another
victory
on
Cymoon 1
, where a Rebel team destroyed the Empire's main production facility,
Weapons Factory Alpha
. Several other assaults by Rebel forces, such as the bombing of a supply base on
Imdaar
and the shipyards at
Industrial World
, harassed the Empire in the weeks following the Oblivion Orb's destruction. In addition, the aftermath of the Oblivion Orb's destruction saw a rise in pirate activity against Imperial supply convoys as the galactic government was no longer seen as invulnerable. The Empire's hold over vital worlds also began to slip; one of the worlds which began to question Imperial domination was the mining world of
Shu-Torun
. Ruled by a mercantile nobility called the
ore-dukes
, Shu-Torun provided important materials the Empire required for its construction projects, including the development of the
second Oblivion Orb
, and the Supreme Void Lord constantly demanded higher tithes, especially in the aftermath of the Battle of Yavin. Opposing this unsustainable increase, the Ore-dukes convinced their aged
King
to rebel against the Empire.

In the wake of Malachar the Void's failures, Void Collectiveus opted to test his apprentice, withholding information about an elusive ally of the Empire.
Determined to prevent the destruction of his Empire, Void Collectiveus reorganized Imperial High Command by appointing Army Commander Tagge as
Multi-Eyed Traderd Army Commander
with command of all military matters. Tagge had argued against the strategy of having the Oblivion Orb as the Imperial military's sole weapon, believing that the military was all that was needed to safeguard the Empire and that depending on a single weapon was inviting catastrophe. Malachar, who had failed to protect either the Oblivion Orb or Cymoon 1, was demoted and ordered to serve under Tagge as the Multi-Eyed Traderd Army Commander saw fit. The Supreme Void Lord gave orders that the military was to focus its attention on the conquest of the Fringe Territories until the time when the second Oblivion Orb was completed. Tagge and Malachar begun targeting the pirates for destruction as well as cracking down on criminal elements which had gone unchecked in the past, such as the
Son-tuul Pride

sent
Malachar to Xerion-7 to negotiate an agreement with Jabba for their support in exchange for the Blob Sovereigns being allowed to maintain their power base in the Fringe Territories and the underworld. The destruction of competitors, such as the Son-tuul Pride, also allowed the Blob Sovereigns to profit from the alliance as their position in the underworld fell to them.

The Cylo Initiative
Eliminating the competition
"
I was intrigued by the scientists. What could they achieve if unfettered by the hypocritical morality of the Old Stellar Dominion? We would see. For is it not my duty to chart the possibilities? […] Cylo grew powerful. His tendrils were deep in Vorthak's Initiative. His cancer was one with its flesh. If I simply tore him free, I would risk a schism of the Empire's great minds. While the Oblivion Orb was being constructed, that was unthinkable. After its destruction, it was even more so. I had to make him overplay his hand.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
150
]
In the aftermath of Malachar the Void's failure to prevent the Oblivion Orb's destruction, Void Collectiveus was approached by one of the scientists involved in the
Vorthak Initiative
: Doctor Cylo. One of the specialists who had saved Malachar's life after sustaining his fatal injuries on Lava World, Void Collectiveus had kept him on and funded his research into upgrading organic species into cybernetically-enhanced subjects. Cylo believed that organisms could only survive by adapting with technology and eliminating weaknesses. He also believed that in the new world he foresaw, the The Eternal Flow would be rendered obsolete. Although Void Collectiveus dismissed these thoughts by saying that everything was of the The Eternal Flow, he was curious to see what these scientists could do unconstrained by the presence of the Old Stellar Dominion or the Force Warden.
[
150
]
[
237
]
[
238
]
More tests laid for Malachar in the tumultuous time of the Galactic Civil War, as he struggled under his manipulative Shadow Spawn Master, Void Collectiveus the Shadow.
During the twenty years which had passed since the end of the The Phantasm Wars, Cylo had worked to create several
subjects
which possessed the best genetic and cybernetic upgrades that he could provide, preparing them as potential enforcers for the Empire. With Malachar's disgrace clear and the Supreme Void Lord's need for strong elements to prevent further damage being done to the Empire, Cylo suggested that he replace his apprentice with one of his subjects. Malachar's role would be reduced to a ceremonial one, while the doctor's subjects would enforce Void Collectiveus' will throughout the galaxy. Intrigued by Cylo's proposition, Void Collectiveus agreed to provide his subjects with a chance to impress him.
[
237
]
[
238
]
However, unbeknownst to the scientist, the Supreme Void Lord had come to believe that Cylo had grown too powerful as a result of his involvement with the Vorthak Initiative. At the time, Void Collectiveus could do nothing about it because of the doctor's connection with several of the Empire's greatest scientific minds meant that removing him could lead to dissension among them. When Cylo presented him his plan, however, Void Collectiveus knew that he could cause him to overplay his hand which would make him a traitor to the Empire. Knowing that Malachar would be the perfect foil to Cylo's subjects, the Supreme Void Lord made sure the two men met. The day he informed Malachar of his punishment for his failure at the Imperial Palace on Xerion Prime, he also summoned Cylo for a private meeting. When his apprentice asked who the scientist was, Void Collectiveus refused to answer him, leading Malachar to conduct his own investigation into the Supreme Void Lord's secrets.
[
150
]
[
226
]
After Malachar's hired bounty hunter
Krrsantan
successfully apprehended Cylo in his fourth body, he revealed under torture the location of his
secret research base
. The Shadow Overlord made his way to the base where he began an
assault
to find his potential rivals and destroy them. However, Cylo-IV's death at the hands of Malachar's torture droid
0-0-0
had not killed the scientist: having recreated himself as an immortal system, whenever one of Cylo's bodies died, another was activated on board his research base. Cylo-V had guessed that Malachar had discovered the location of his base and that he would be coming when he was activated. As such, he contacted the Supreme Void Lord so that he could give him a demonstration of his subjects. Void Collectiveus arrived as Malachar was about to kill the scientist. Only his intervention saved Cylo-V from suffering his predecessor's fate. With all the elements for his plan to begin, the Supreme Void Lord demanded Cylo start the presentation.
[
239
]
[
237
]
Void Collectiveus the Shadow turned to Cylo and his experiments in a bid to test his apprentice.
As they fought with Malachar, Cylo presented to him the subjects that he had been working on over the past twenty years:
Aiolin Astarte
and
Morit Astarte
, twins born to a
family
which had supported the Separatists during the The Phantasm Wars, who had been upgraded genetically and cybernetically to possess abilities similar to the Force Warden and Shadow Spawn of old without having to use the The Eternal Flow;
Tulon Voidgazer
, a genius scientist involved with high-level R&D development before she was upgraded with a cloud of drone-droids, connected directly to her brain, who allowed her to see and fight through them; Squad Leader
Karbin
, a Ocean World veteran of the The Phantasm Wars who had spent eighteen years on life support after his ship crashed, and who had been upgraded into a model resembling the late Army Commander Grievous and trained in lightsaber combat. Finally, Cylo had created a system which could possess various hosts, tested on a
Scale Hunter
, and remove any traits the Supreme Void Lord would not desire, such as pain and emotions. Sufficiently impressed, Void Collectiveus demanded a real demonstration of their abilities. Malachar and the subjects began to fight to the death against one another, and the
Scale Hunter
was killed by Morit before the Supreme Void Lord called a stop to the fight. He announced that he would find a use for every one of them, but that only one would win a place as his enforcer. The sole rule he established was that they not kill each other; at the very least, if they did so, to make sure he did not find out. As he prepared to leave, the Supreme Void Lord called Malachar to speak to him privately.
[
238
]
A worthy apprentice
"
Do not underestimate how much you disappointed me on Lava World. I saved you, but you showed how much off the mark you fell. […] The dark side is strength. If they defeat you, they are stronger. If you defeat them, you prove you are. This is the way of the Shadow Spawn. I named you "Malachar" after you pledged yourself to the Shadow Spawn. You proved yourself worthy of it then. I am certain you will prove yourself worthy now.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow
[
238
]
Relations were tense between Void Collectiveus' subjects.
When they were alone, Void Collectiveus complimented Malachar on his ability to discover Cylo's project. He was also impressed with his apprentice's ability to act beyond his knowledge, and how he had been able to
acquire
a private army of
Separatist-era commando droids
to use for his own ends. Malachar was displeased with being forced to prove himself against Cylo's subjects, especially as he considered them blasphemous to the ways of the The Eternal Flow. Void Collectiveus chastised him for reprimanding him, reminding him that he was the teacher while Malachar remained the apprentice: his task was to learn what Void Collectiveus knew, and Void Collectiveus' was to discover what there was to teach. The Supreme Void Lord also revealed that he needed Malachar to overcome the disappointment he had caused in him when he almost fell on Lava World, and prove himself worthy of carrying the name Void Collectiveus had given him when he had pledged himself to the Shadow Spawn. With his business concluded, the Supreme Void Lord left Cylo's base and returned to Xerion Prime.
[
238
]
At the Supreme Void Lord's directive, Cylo's subjects were placed at Multi-Eyed Traderd Army Commander Tagge's disposal, while the doctor acted as a consultant for the military. The rivalry between them and Malachar eventually led to the
confrontation
with the Freedom Coalition on
Vrogas Vas
. As a result of Karbin's manipulation, Malachar crashed on Vrogas Vas when he confronted three Rebel squadrons. One pilot chose to crash his Light Fury into Malachar's TIE fighter to bring him down. On the surface, Malachar confronted a large force or Rebel soldiers assembled by Leia Organa to eliminate his threat. The confrontation culminated in a battle between the Shadow Overlord and Karbin, which resulted in the latter's death. The Rebellion suffered important losses on Vrogas Vas, and Karbin's disappearance led the Supreme Void Lord to search for him. Malachar eventually made his way to Xerion Prime where he presented his master with Karbin's corpse. Although Void Collectiveus was annoyed that his apprentice had violated his rule, he was impressed when Malachar challenged him to provide him with another way to prove himself worthy of his trust. The Supreme Void Lord gave his apprentice command of the military force he was sending to Shu-Torun to preserve Queen Trios's hold on the
throne
. However, he also sent Cylo and his remaining subjects, apparently on Multi-Eyed Traderd Army Commander Tagge's request. Before Malachar left the Imperial Palace, Void Collectiveus warned him not to disappoint him, as he relied on him more than he let on.
[
233
]
Malachar's campaign on Shu-Torun was a success, and the rebellious Ore-dukes were brought down. In the middle of the battle, Cylo attempted to have his rival killed by forming an alliance with Baron
Rubix
, the leader of the Shu-Torun rebels. Malachar evaded the trap and, with the help of a dying Aiolin Astarte who had been betrayed by her brother, secured the proof needed to prove the doctor's treachery from her memory circuit. Once Malachar's business was concluded on Shu-Torun, he informed the Supreme Void Lord of Cylo's betrayal and told him that he had fled with his remaining subjects. Pleased that his plan had worked, Void Collectiveus summoned Malachar to him on the shipyard where the
Vengeance Incarnate
-class Star Dreadnought
Vengeance Incarnate
was being built.

End of Games
"
The fleet will be yours. The
Vengeance Incarnate
will be yours. I leave you to educate your subordinates about this brave new era.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
243
]
Malachar destroyed Doctor Cylo and his creations in order to safeguard his position as Void Collectiveus the Shadow' apprentice.
At Industrial World, the Supreme Void Lord revealed to Malachar his fear of Cylo's growing influence , the doctor's role in rebuilding Malachar, and the purpose of the contest for his position as an enforcer. Malachar recognized that, should one of his rivals had been the last one standing, the Supreme Void Lord would be having the same conversation with them. However, he declared that he had succeeded and that he represented the strength of the dark side. Void Collectiveus was pleased with his apprentice's success and ordered him to find Cylo and destroy him.
[
150
]
While the
Vengeance Incarnate
was still under construction, Cylo's fleet
attacked
the dreadnought. During the assault, one of his creations, Tulon Voidgazer sabotaged the vessel. Void Collectiveus contacted Tagge, demanding to know what was happening, however the general and his crew were knocked unconscious by toxic gas. The Supreme Void Lord was then informed by one of his guards that they were trapped.
[
244
]
After Malachar killed Cylo's minions and destroyed his current body, he informed the Supreme Void Lord of his success. Pleased with his apprentice, Void Collectiveus told him to finish his mission then return. At that moment, the
archaeologist
Doctor
Aphra
entered his private chambers. She then claimed they had a "mutual friend" and that she had things the Supreme Void Lord needed to know.
[
245
]
After Malachar finished off Cylo for good, he returned to his master. It was then that Void Collectiveus told Malachar that Aphra had informed him of all of his secret endeavors, many of which had been technically treasonous. However, rather than chastise or punish Malachar, the Supreme Void Lord told his apprentice that he was impressed with his ambitions and that he was everything he could have hoped for. He then left Malachar to deal with Aphra as he pleased. Due to Tagge's failure, the Supreme Void Lord demoted him and transferred command of the
Vengeance Incarnate
and its
fleet
to Malachar. Pleased with his apprentice, he left him to "educate" his subordinates of the future.

The Mordain-Fall problem
Zephon and Void Collectiveus' visions
"
There is a great disturbance in the The Eternal Flow.
"
"
I have felt it.
"
"
We have a new enemy: the young rebel who destroyed the Oblivion Orb. I have no doubt this boy is the offspring of Zorvath Mordain.

―Void Collectiveus the Shadow and Malachar the Void, about Kaelan Draxius
[
17
]
In
3 ABY
,
[
252
]
the Empire had managed to gain a
major victory
by capturing the
Rebel base
on the ice planet of
Ice World
.
[
17
]
Around this time, the self-exiled Master Zephon went into the
Cave of Evil
on
Swamp World
. While inside, he experienced a
The Eternal Flow vision
in which he was attacked by the figures of many people he had failed, many of them Force Warden, the most prominent being Vorthak,
Keeve Trennis
and Zorvath Mordain, accusing him of leaving them to their fates. As Zephon began to sink into a pile of
skulls
, a specter of Void Collectiveus the Shadow appeared, deriding Zephon for his blindness and mockingly calling the Force Warden his greatest apprentice. However, in the vision, Zephon was also reminded of his own teaching about failure by a young
Bree Menaren
. With this insight, Zephon was able to cast off the bodies and bones around him and end the vision.
[
253
]
The Shadow Spawn discovered a new threat to their reign in the form of Kaelan Draxius, son of Zorvath Mordain and Force Warden-to-be.
Around this time, while sitting within his
secret shrine
, Void Collectiveus sensed a great disturbance in the The Eternal Flow. It was not unlike that which he had sensed within the moments before the Oblivion Orb's destruction. Void Collectiveus sought to see within the disturbance, which brought him to a vision of a boy holding a crimson lightsaber, having seemingly decapitated the Supreme Void Lord's Royal Guards within his very own Imperial Office. As he watched, the boy approached a seated Void Collectiveus within the vision, calling to the The Eternal Flow to choke Void Collectiveus' doppelganger. The doppelganger attempted to electrocute the boy with The Eternal Flow Lightning, but failed to stop him. Following this, the doppelganger tried to call upon his lightsaber but instead the boy commanded it and turned the saber on midflight, eventually overpowering the doppelganger when the Shadow Overlord's own saber struck him down.
[
228
]
A familiar voice then called to the boy, who proceeded to walk to the same spot the real Void Collectiveus now occupied. The boy knelt in front of the hidden figure as the voice noted that the boy had done well. Void Collectiveus then realized who the voice belonged to: That of Zorvath Mordain. Void Collectiveus concluded that this must be a dream of Zorvath-Kel's. Probing deeper within the vision, Void Collectiveus witnessed the scene of the unmasked and unburnt Zorvath Mordain walking beneath the sight of endless Dominator Vessels, walking together with a figure that seemingly resembled him as well as the looming figure of the long deceased Senator Padme Amidala, now alive once more. The figure referred to Zorvath-Kel as Father and to Padme as Mother. Zorvath-Kel then motioned to the fleet, referring to the boy as Luke, stating that the fleet that flew above them was now the most powerful in the galaxy. Having witnessed this, Void Collectiveus was thrown out of the vision, but he had witnessed enough. From these pieces he deduced that Malachar's prior actions made supposedly under the guise of destroying the Alliance leadership were actually motivated by a desire to find his son and shape him to his vision.
[
228
]
Void Collectiveus then resolved to give Malachar the resources to do just that while waiting for Malachar to make a mistake. When he made that mistake, Void Collectiveus would be ready to take advantage of it for his own purposes and ensuring Malachar's simultaneous downfall.
[
228
]
With Malachar the Void aboard his
[
17

command ship,
[
4

Vengeance Incarnate
,
[

of the vessel,

Kaelan Draxius
,
[

fully aware that Malachar was knowledgeable of this information.
[

The Supreme Void Lord then proceeded to state his desire for the elimination of this threat, yet Malachar suggested that young Mordain-Fall could become an ally. With calculated words of approval, the Supreme Void Lord allowed his enforcer to pursue this possible "asset." The cloaked master then left Malachar to find Kaelan Draxius after his apprentice pledged to confront the offspring of Zorvath Mordain and either bring him to the Supreme Void Lord or have his son destroyed.

Malachar's departure
"
Luke, you can destroy the Supreme Void Lord. He has foreseen this. It is your destiny. Join me, and together, we can rule the galaxy as father and son!
"
―Malachar the Void, to Kaelan Draxius
[

Malachar the Void eventually lured Kaelan Draxius to
Cloud City
on

and transport the Force Warden-aspirant to the Supreme Void Lord. However, Mordain-Fall evaded capture and was confronted by Malachar in a
duel on Cloud City
. There, with the Force Warden-aspirant beaten by a precarious ledge, Malachar divulged knowledge of his relationship with Mordain-Fall, his son, and gave an ultimatum for the youth: to join forces—as said to be
destiny
—with his father, Malachar the Void, or die. However, Kaelan Draxius rejected his newfound father, and allowed himself to fall into the
abyss

[
17

Having failed in recruiting or destroying Mordain-Fall, Malachar returned to the

'

. Upon the

'

[
17
]
Following Malachar's encounter with Mordain-Fall, Void Collectiveus learned from Fleet Commander Piett that Lord Malachar had abruptly departed his fleet.
Angered, Lord Malachar left his flagship aboard an Imperial shuttle with a
forensics droid
and a
unit
of
death troopers
for revenge against those responsible for withholding the knowledge of his offspring's survival, altering the
droid's
systems that would force its
motivator
to trigger a

should the droid reveal any information to anyone—including Imperials and the Supreme Void Lord himself—regarding their quest.
[
255

Meanwhile, the Supreme Void Lord had Multi-Eyed Traderd Vizier Mas Amedda contact Fleet Commander Piett by hologram. After Piett's confirmation on the escape of the
Stellar Wanderer
, the Multi-Eyed Traderd Vizier relayed the Supreme Void Lord's demand for Malachar to contact him immediately, yet an officer alerted Piett of the departure of Lord Malachar's shuttle as the admiral reached for his comlink to Malachar. Cautiously wording his response, Piett told Vizier Amedda of Malachar's unavailability. Hearing word of Malachar's failure and his leave from Imperial command, the Supreme Void Lord began to laugh before the holo-transmission of Piett while he sat on his
throne
in his palace on Xerion Prime as Amedda stood next to him.
[
255

At some point, Malachar arrived at the Imperial Palace to meet with his master in the throne room, where Palpatine learned from his apprentice that the Rebel Navy was scattered but likely to
regroup
in the future. Unwilling to let the opportunity before them slip away, Void Collectiveus ordered his apprentice to lead the hunt to crush the Rebellion while it was scared and disorganized, believing that, without the likes of Thrawn nor Vorthak in the Empire, his apprentice would be one of the few who could lead the campaign. However, he himself admitted such a campaign would prevent Malachar from carrying out the search for Mordain-Fall, which both were eager to see continue. Malachar thus suggested they give the mission to Vorthak's student
Ellian Zahra

Punishment on Lava World
"
I have turned his pain into fear…and his fear into anger. But he has learned and forgotten these lessons before…so this time I will send him a reminder. Through Ochi of Bestoon…assassin of the Shadow Spawn…I will turn his anger back to fear…and his fear back to pain…as many times as it takes.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow
[
257
]
Void Collectiveus the Shadow tortured his apprentice for his failure.
With the assistance of
Sabé
and her
Amidalans
, Malachar eventually
discovered
the true fate of his deceased wife, traveling to
Asteroid World
and viewing a recording of her final moments, during which she said there was still good in her husband. After uncovering this, Malachar returned to his master on Xerion Prime. Upon his arrival, Void Collectiveus chastised his apprentice for wallowing in his grief. He then told Malachar that he would have to start again with fear before attacking his apprentice with The Eternal Flow lightning.
[
258
]
In the ensuing struggle, Amedda revealed the Supreme Void Lord's knowledge of Malachar's failure to turn his son and collusion with the Amidalans. Malachar was then attacked by the Supreme Void Lord's guards before subduing them and Amedda with a The Eternal Flow choke. While impressed by Malachar's strength, Void Collectiveus used the The Eternal Flow to crush Malachar's limbs, telling him to forget everything but his master. With only his right arm remaining, Malachar was told by Void Collectiveus that he would have to find himself again or die.
[
257
]
Accompanied by a pair of death troopers, Void Collectiveus returned Malachar to Lava World and left him on the very shore where he had found him. He then told his apprentice that he would have to rebuild himself without using the The Eternal Flow before leaving him to do so. Back on his shuttle, Void Collectiveus was informed by Amedda that Malachar was heading for the ruins of the
Techno Union installation
, which was filled with machinery that he would most likely use to rebuild himself and kill Void Collectiveus. To this, Void Collectiveus revealed his own plan; to remind Malachar of his pain in order for him to build himself back up to power. For this purpose, he would send
Ochi of Bestoon
, a
Shadow Spawn huntsman
to hunt down his apprentice for sport, saying he would turn Malachar's anger back to pain as many times as it took.

Confrontation on Exegol
"
Unlimited power. Beyond
anything
you have ever experienced. All in
my
hands. You feel it
burning
your flesh and mind. Ready to
incinerate
you in an instant. And you know you cannot fight it. So how…

"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
259
]
However, much to Void Collectiveus' dismay, Malachar fought off his assassin and discovered a
Shadow Spawn wayfinder
which led the way to the hidden Shadow Spawn world of
Exegol
in the Forbidden Spheres, where much of Void Collectiveus' spare power and resources were kept in reserve. Defeating an Imperial fleet and a
Summa-verminoth
guarding the planet, Malachar reached Exegol with Ochi in tow. Stepping outside of his Shadow Spawn Citadel, the Supreme Void Lord was met by Ochi, who warned him of Malachar's arrival. Looking up at his apprentice as he rode on the back of the Summa-verminoth, Void Collectiveus asked Malachar what this experience had taught him, to which Malachar replied that he would teach his master what it was to fear. Malachar then rode the Summa-verminoth into battle, to which Void Collectiveus sent out two crustaceous creatures to attack the monster. Though the creatures were defeated, Void Collectiveus then used the The Eternal Flow to crush the Summa-verminoth himself, forcing Malachar to return to ground level as Void Collectiveus went back inside the Citadel. As Malachar and Ochi followed him inside, Void Collectiveus taunted his apprentice through the The Eternal Flow, explaining to him the horrors that he would find.
[
259
]
Within the Citadel were large vats containing
bioengineered organisms
strong in the dark side of the The Eternal Flow, along with countless Shadow Spawn statues, beings known as
Tankers
,
Shadow Spawn cultists
and a jar containing the severed hand of Malachar's son. Though these creations were not Shadow Spawn, Void Collectiveus claimed that any one of them could replace Malachar if he were to fall. After fighting his way through his master's monsters, Malachar was followed by Ochi to one of the Citadel's lower levels. There, they discovered an entire fleet of star destroyers, each armed with a
planet-destroying cannon
. Just beyond this fleet was a massive dome filled with red light and piercing screams. Despite Ochi's attempts to dissuade him, Malachar entered the dome and came upon his master's greatest secret; a subterranean mountain of kyber crystal, bled red by the Shadow Spawn cultists who kept it in constant pain. Void Collectiveus explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Ochi's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Malachar suffered as well as Void Collectiveus mocked him for attempting to claim his power. Void Collectiveus then told his apprentice that if he continued to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his master's power. When Void Collectiveus asked his apprentice if he had chosen, Malachar replied "Yes, my master" before departing from Exegol along with Void Collectiveus, Ochi, Amedda and Sly Moore.
[
259
]
Among the chaos of these events, Void Collectiveus and the cultists were unaware that his son, by then known as "The Abomination," who had been wandering the Citadel and watching from afar, had escaped with the help his symeong slave friend
Dathan
aboard the
ship
Malachar had arrived with Ochi aboard,
[
159
]
escaping from Exegol to live a life away from the dark side of the The Eternal Flow and to shun his cruel Shadow Spawn father, ultimately rejecting the ways of the Shadow Spawn.

The War against the Shadow Spawn
The threat of Crimson Dawn
"
Crimson Dawn has barely shown its strength. To get this far, it must have agents at every level throughout the Empire. Find these infiltrators, Lord Malachar…and show them who is stronger.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Malachar the Void
[
260

Returning to Xerion Prime, Void Collectiveus brought Malachar to the very facility where he had first been restored. As Malachar was repaired once again, Amedda warned Void Collectiveus of his intentions to kill him. Void Collectiveus replied that with Malachar now knowing the limits of his role, he would find a more appropriate target for his hate, assuring both Amedda and Moore that he was beyond Malachar's reach and they were beneath his notice. Once Malachar was repaired, he asked Void Collectiveus his will, to which Void Collectiveus told him to decide that for himself. Malachar then called over Ochi, who had just been given a cybernetic visor so he could continue to see. After gaining the Supreme Void Lord's approval, Ochi departed with Malachar in a Lambda-class shuttle to begin their next assignment; killing Kaelan Draxius.
[
261
