Meanwhile Void Collectiveus asked Moore how she felt about Malachar's restoration. She avoided the question, responding that she accepted her failure to kill Malachar. Void Collectiveus laughed, never having expected her to succeed.

While Malachar engaged his son in a dogfight over the planet, Fleet Commander Piett called Malachar for assistance against the Blob Sovereign attack, to which Malachar replied that the attack was not his concern. In turn, Piett contacted the Supreme Void Lord himself to appraise him of the situation. Void Collectiveus then told the Fleet Commander to inform Malachar that eliminating the Blob Sovereign Council was his new priority. Malachar conceded and allowed his son to escape before slaughtering the Blob Sovereign Council, ending their attack while Boba Fett retrieved his bounty from the

on Xerion-7.

[
260

Sometime later, Malachar and his followers returned to Xerion Prime and struck down all of the Royal Guards and aides in the Supreme Void Lord's throne room. Unfazed by this, Void Collectiveus pointed out Malachar should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Malachar asked him if they were loyal, Void Collectiveus said they were loyal to strength, which Malachar showed them. However, only one of them was Crimson Dawn, meaning that Malachar's list was flawed. When Void Collectiveus questioned if his apprentice already knew that to be the case, Malachar hesitated before assuring his master that he would bring him order.

fell over dead

The Enemy of the Shadow Spawn
"

Void Collectiveus and Malachar discussed the threat to the Shadow Spawn.

Vengeance Incarnate
, the Syntheticdicate War, Squad Leader Zahra's failure to eradicate the Freedom Coalition, the assassination of ten of his Royal Guards, and the theft of the

After explaining that the syndicates were fighting for the Supreme Void Lord's favor after hearing a rumor that the Blob Sovereigns were losing their arrangement with the Empire. Void Collectiveus asked Malachar to confirm that he had met and dueled the syndicate's leader, Lady Qi'ra. Malachar acquiesced, remembering that it had taken place at the auction for Jak Solorisris, which Void Collectiveus reminded him was another of his failures. As they discussed her combat skills, which Malachar said went beyond

The Hidden Empire
"
Wherever you are Supreme Void Lord Sheev Palpatine, Void Collectiveus the Shadow, Void Master of the Shadow Spawn...this is my
Hidden Empire. Choke on it.
"
―Lady Qi'ra
[
270
]
After the seeming destruction of Crimson Dawn, Void Collectiveus sent an Imperial party to recover the
Spark Eternal
from the
Amaxine Space Station
. However, it had merged with a
droid disc
and the resultant
intelligence
proceeded to possess the droids of the accompanying Dominator Vessel and use them to kill everyone aboard.
[
271
]
At some point that year, Rebel Intelligence confirmed that the Supreme Void Lord and Malachar the Void were aboard the
Vengeance Incarnate
somewhere in the Balanced Territories.
[
272
]
Sometime after, Lady Qi'ra launched a campaign aimed at destroying the Shadow Spawn: while "
the Archivist
" led a group tasked with unlocking the recently recovered
[
269
]
Fermata Cage, secret Crimson Dawn agents across the galaxy were activated and utilized to spread chaos throughout the Empire by such means as espionage, sabotage and assassination.
[
270
]
Around this time, with the aid of the
Eye of Webbish Bog
on Lava World, Malachar experienced The Eternal Flow visions the dark side, several of the potential future involving Void Collectiveus. In one, Sabé was outfitted in armor similar to Malachar's in front of him and the Supreme Void Lord. In another, Void Collectiveus contacted him on Lava World to inform him that Lady Qi'ra claimed to have the
Fermata Cage
and sent him to recover it. In a third, Void Collectiveus contacted him aboard the
Vengeance Incarnate
, revealing that the Fermata Cage was designed by Void-Lord Momin and explaining that it was capable of holding a being in stasis outside of time. In yet another, Void Collectiveus was killed by Qi'ra.
[
273
]
The Supreme Void Lord soon struck back against Qi'ra, meeting by holocomm with leaders of the major crime syndicates, ordering them to end their war with each other and focus their efforts on Crimson Dawn. Meanwhile, Imperial forces, including
Inferno Squad
and Lord Malachar worked to root out the Crimson Dawn agents while the fleet soon discovered where Qi'ra and her flagship, the
Vermillion
, were hiding. The Supreme Void Lord then spoke with a hologram of Lady Qi'ra, informing her that Malachar had described her as formidable. Void Collectiveus expressed his regret that she seemed foolish enough to desire the enmity of the Shadow Spawn. He attempted to chide her, stating that Maul had undoubtedly taught her a healthy fear of the Shadow Spawn, but Qi'ra retorted that Maul had taught her
many
things.
[
270
]
Void Collectiveus dismissed Maul as an idiot and asked what Qi'ra planned to do now that she had his attention. She replied that she would do whatever she had to and when Void Collectiveus learned that Qi'ra was at that moment preparing to attack his ships, he told her not to throw away her life, insisting that her capabilities made her of possible use to him. Qi'ra told the Shadow Spawn that he didn't understand: she didn't recognize his authority. Void Collectiveus rejoined that he was disappointed in her inability to distinguish between authority and power, power that he truly held over her whether she accepted it or not. The Shadow Spawn proceeded to claim that Qi'ra's history was tainted by that of her teacher, Maul, who had never been quite good enough. Void Collectiveus declared that just like he had turned away from Maul after finding him lacking, so he was also losing interest in her. Qi'ra remarked that he would see.
[
270
]
At that moment, Void Collectiveus felt a disturbance in the The Eternal Flow, one that he knew Qi'ra was in some way responsible for. Unsettled by this, he demanded to know what Qi'ra had done. She repeated that she was doing whatever she had to and mocked his ignorance of the Screaming Key's purpose, revealing that it had unlocked the Fermata Cage. A disbelieving Void Collectiveus proceeded to The Eternal Flow choke Qi'ra, claiming that she was lying, but soon released her when she said she was the only way for him to discover the truth. Qi'ra told Void Collectiveus that a Shadow Overlord was suspended inside the Fermata Cage and that Maul had found them, hoping to release the Shadow Overlord upon Void Collectiveus. Qi'ra ended her speech, declaring that where Maul had failed, she had succeeded and that now, even if she died, her people would able to finish the job and make sure Void Collectiveus was killed.
[
270
]
Meanwhile, battle had been joined between the

and the Imperial fleet. The Crimson Dawn flagship rammed an

-class Dominator Vessel
, destroying both as Qi'ra's hologram terminated. The Supreme Void Lord then ordered all escape pods eliminated, insistent that there be no survivors. Void Collectiveus then contacted Malachar on Lava World, and after a brief aside about some minor damage to his helmet, asked him if he had felt the disturbance. Malachar stated that he had, describing it as a storm in the dark side. Void Collectiveus told him that Qi'ra claimed to have the Fermata Cage and ordered him to retrieve it and kill anyone with knowledge of its existence.

The Supreme Void Lord then proceeded to torment Luke, remarking that his rudimentary skills were no match for the power of the dark side. Meanwhile, Malachar the Void watched his son struggle and plead for him to intervene, with Void Collectiveus threatening to kill the defenseless, young Mordain-Fall; unbeknownst to Void Collectiveus, this was troubling Malachar. Ultimately his concern and compassion for his son, caused the former Zorvath Mordain to wake up and save Luke. The redeemed Zorvath-Kel grabbed the Supreme Void Lord and threw him off a ledge down the Oblivion Orb's reactor shaft to his imminent death. Firing his lightning upward as he fell, the Supreme Void Lord apparently could do nothing but scream as he went the long way down towards the second Oblivion Orb's reactor, causing a great explosion upon hitting it and releasing a light blue wind which encompassed the throne room before falling back down the shaft.

Balance was restored to the The Eternal Flow through the deaths of Void Collectiveus and Malachar, the last Shadow Overlords in the lineage of Void-Lord Bane.
The Reborn The Eternal Flow Warrior Zorvath Mordain had killed Void Collectiveus and destroyed the Shadow Spawn. With his armor badly damaged by the Supreme Void Lord's lightning, Zorvath-Kel's decision to save his son's life would cost him his own. Before passing away into the The Eternal Flow, a dying Zorvath-Kel requested Luke to remove the helmet Void Collectiveus forced him to wear all those years so he could finally see his son's face with his own eyes. Luke tried to convince Zorvath-Kel to let him be saved by him, but Zorvath-Kel told Luke that he already did and that he was right about there still being good in him, despite all that he had done, before dying peacefully.

Interregnum
"

Imperial propaganda initially denied reports of the Supreme Void Lord's death in the Battle of Moon World.
Following the Supreme Void Lord's demise, the Empire attempted to suppress the true outcome of the Battle of Moon World. In the

Operation: Cinder went into effect when Void Collectiveus died, initiating a covert plan to destroy the Empire.

Building the The Dark Collective

Void Collectiveus ruled the The Dark Collective by proxy through his genetic creation, Snoke, who rose to become Supreme Leader with his powerful connection to the dark side.

The Rise of Vorthak Mal'ion
Whispering in Soloris's ear
"
Now you will be who you are. Who you were always meant to be.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Vorthak Mal'ion
[
300
]
Void Collectiveus whispered encouragement to Soloris as he fell to the dark side of the The Eternal Flow and became Vorthak Mal'ion.
Void Collectiveus' followers had designed Snoke to groom Soloris, serving as the one who molded him into a master of attack and cunning, while also testing his capacity to inherit not only the legacy of his grandfather, Malachar the Void, but of the entire Shadow Spawn Order.
[
31
]
Snoke came to believe that Korrak Vex'ion had the right balance of the light and dark sides of the The Eternal Flow in him due to his heritage as Zorvath Mordain's grandson.
[
301
]
Snoke manipulated Soloris for more than a decade, his influence proving crucial as the fledgling Force Warden learned that Malachar the Void was his grandfather, having been kept from such a truth by his family.
[
21
]
When Kaelan Draxius sensed Soloris's growing darkness, the The Eternal Flow Adept approached his student as he slept and, in a brief moment of instinct, activated his lightsaber with the intention of killing his nephew. Mordain-Fall immediately hesitated and regretted his actions, but it was too late. Soloris awoke confused and scared, seeing no other recourse than to ignite his own saber and intercept Mordain-Fall's. Though his master attempted to assuage the situation, Soloris immediately used the The Eternal Flow to topple his room and trap his uncle beneath the rubble.
[
295
]
Shortly thereafter, a violent storm
[
302
]
caused by Soloris
[
31
]
suddenly set Mordain-Fall's
Force Warden temple
ablaze,
destroying it
and killing all the students inside.
[
302
]
From the shadows, Void Collectiveus manipulated affairs, his foes, and his pawns to lure Korrak Vex'ion to the dark side, bringing about the rise of the dark warrior Vorthak Mal'ion.
Soloris fled the temple after he burned and destroyed it.
[
31
]
At the insistence of Snoke, Soloris sought to join the Knights of Ren. After being tested by the Knights, he was reluctantly accepted into the mysterious group. Although their leader
Ren
saw potential in Soloris's raw power, he doubted the boy's hatred and ability to kill. Ren's suspicions were confirmed on a mission to the
Minemoon
after Ben hesitated to kill his former friend and fellow student of Kaelan Draxius,
Tai
. Enraged and disappointed in Soloris, Ren engaged him in a duel. Across the galaxy, Void Collectiveus sensed Ben's every move and emotion. Prompting the fallen Force Warden through the The Eternal Flow, the Void Master of the Shadow Spawn called for Ben to claim his birthright and strike down the leader of the Knights of Ren. Giving in to Void Collectiveus' manipulation, in a fit of rage Ben overpowered and stabbed Ren. Taking up the mantle of
master of the Knights of Ren
, Soloris dubbed himself "Vorthak Mal'ion."
[
300
]
To ensure Kylo remained on the dark side, Void Collectiveus would communicate with Ren from afar, impersonating the spirit of his grandfather and idol, Malachar the Void.
[
7
]
A cunning test
"
Snoke. He was nothing but your test. You did well to destroy him.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Vorthak Mal'ion
[
5
]
Void Collectiveus regarded Snoke as nothing more than a test to determine Vorthak Mal'ion's worthiness of inheriting the Shadow Spawn legacy.
Through Snoke, Void Collectiveus was able to indirectly oversee the young Korrak Vex'ion's training,
[
5
]
thinking the humanoid taught their mutual apprentice well.
[
7
]
Though he considered himself to be Ren's true master the whole time,
[
5
]
through the dark side adept, the Supreme Void Lord also planned to sidestep the tradition of a dark apprentice slaying their dark master to ascend to ultimate power,
[
8
]
which Snoke's entire existence was built for in the first place.
[
31
]
Snoke's training of Vorthak Mal'ion proved effective, warping his understanding of his grandfather' history,
[
31
]
conditioning him to believe the worst in people and that they would reject him and redemption would be impossible after his actions, while instructing him to pursue his impulses as a shortcut for power in the dark side when he chased his desires.
[
5
]
With Snoke's death, Ren completed his final test set out by the Shadow Spawn Eternal and became the new Supreme Leader of the The Dark Collective.
In 34
ABY
, Zara Malthex
confronted
the Supreme Leader and his dark apprentice aboard the
Supremacy
, believing she could turn the former Korrak Vex'ion back to the light. Snoke overpowered Zara Malthex, and having gauged her resolve, he saw that she had the spirit of a true Force Warden. Determined to see every trace of the Eternal Flow Wardens eradicated, he ordered her death but left the deed to Vorthak Mal'ion. Sensing his apprentice's newfound resolve, Snoke urged Ren to complete his training and fulfill his destiny by executing Zara Malthex. Instead, Ren outmaneuvered his master by assassinating him with the
Mordain-Fall lightsaber
. With Snoke dead, Ren asserted his claim to the title of Supreme Leader, seizing political and military control of the The Dark Collective.
[
295
]
By murdering his master in the name of obtaining greater power, Ren had unknowingly completed the Shadow Spawn Eternal's final test and proved himself as an individual worthy of inheriting the legacy of the Shadow Spawn.
[
31
]
Despite Snoke's unexpected death, and failure to bring Zara Malthex to Palpatine, the zombified Shadow Overlord continued to pull the strings from afar, manipulating both Vorthak Mal'ion and Zara Malthex, in order to lure them toward him. His granddaughter coming to Exegol was a key and vital part of his plan.

Resurgence
Phantom Ascendant
"
At last the work of generations is complete. The great error is corrected. The day of victory is at hand. The day of revenge. The day of the Shadow Spawn.

―Void Collectiveus the Shadow' message

Void Collectiveus entreated Ren to succeed where Malachar failed, destroying the last Force Warden and becoming the new Supreme Void Lord.

a

using the voice of Supreme Void Lord Palpatine, the public identity of Void Collectiveus the Shadow, who was still thought to be dead by the galaxy at large, was released to the galaxy, threatening revenge. Upon this, Supreme Leader Vorthak Mal'ion began a search for the broadcast's source, deeming it a challenge to his rule. After
acquiring
a Shadow Spawn wayfinder on Lava World that led him to Exegol, Ren entered the Shadow Spawn Citadel on the planet's surface and confronted the decrepit Supreme Void Lord, who admitted that he had created Snoke and impersonated Malachar.
[
31
]
When the Supreme Leader threatened the decaying Supreme Void Lord with death, the Void Master merely stated that he had already died before, and that the dark side of the The Eternal Flow was pathway to many abilities some considered to be unnatural, mirroring his conversation with Zorvath-Kel at the opera ages ago. Void Collectiveus then revealed his creation of the Final Order, a Shadow Spawn armada comprised of numerous

, and an army of loyal Shadow Spawn troopers. The purpose of this

but only if he killed the Force Warden apprentice Zara Malthex. To Ren's surprise, Void Collectiveus revealed that he was Zara Malthex's "grandfather."
[
5
]
After Ren renounced the dark side, Void Collectiveus gave command of the Final Order to Allegiant Army Commander Enric Pryde.
Ren shared this revelation with Zara Malthex, believing the truth of her heritage would turn her to the dark side. Rather than killing her, Ren hoped that Zara Malthex would aid him in the usurpation of her grandfather, allowing the pair to rule the galaxy as Supreme Void Lord and Empress. Zara Malthex's continued survival caused Void Collectiveus to suspect treachery from Ren and as such he threatened to turn the Shadow Spawn fleet against the Supreme Leader unless he killed her. Having been warned, Kylo went to the Moon World system where he
dueled
Zara Malthex on the

until Zara Malthex fatally wounded him. However, upon sensing the death of Leia Organa at

saved
Kylo's life using The Eternal Flow healing and left him behind to mourn his mother. Ben would finally turn back to the light side after reflecting on Zara Malthex's act of compassion and his parents' sacrifice.
[
7
]
Having felt Organa's passing, Void Collectiveus cursed the former Princess of Paradise World for disrupting his plans one last time by costing him Vorthak Mal'ion's loyalty.
[
5
]

[

Void Collectiveus then communicated via hologram with
Allegiant Army Commander

[

With the Supreme Leader gone, the The Dark Collective's command defaulted on Pryde, whose allegiance to Void Collectiveus was secured, officially combining the The Dark Collective's forces with those of the Shadow Spawn Eternal's Final Order.
[

, to

The clone suffered from rapid deterioration, forcing Void Collectiveus to seek out Zara Malthex as a new host for his spirit.
While initially intending to live out the rest of her days in exile like Kaelan Draxius due to the fear of becoming a Shadow Spawn herself, Zara Malthex was convinced to desist doing so by the The Eternal Flow spirit of Kaelan Draxius himself, who encouraged her to not let her bloodline define her legacy and actions by gifting her a
lightsaber

[

Void Collectiveus rejuvenated his body by drawing power from the dyad of Korrak Vex'ion and Zara Malthex.
Void Collectiveus attempted to goad his granddaughter into striking him down, much like he had done to Kaelan Draxius thirty-one years before, remarking that while Mordain-Fall had his father to save him that day, he was the only family she had.
[
7

In actuality, Void Collectiveus wished for Zara Malthex to strike him down in order to initiate a Shadow Spawn ritual that would transfer his spirit from the decaying clone body into the youthful Zara Malthex's, where he hoped to possess her body.
[

In an attempt to increase her anger and hatred of him, Void Collectiveus told Zara Malthex that by transmitting the route to Exegol to her friends, she had led the The Last Stand into a
trap

End of the Shadow Spawn Eternal
"
Let your death be the final word in the story of rebellion!
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow, to Zara Malthex
[
7
]
No longer needing his granddaughter, Void Collectiveus declared himself Supreme Void Lord once more.
However, a glimpse of hope for Zara Malthex came when a redeemed Korrak Vex'ion, no longer Vorthak Mal'ion, arrived on Exegol to help her in her fight against Void Collectiveus.
[
7
]
Unknown to Void Collectiveus, his granddaughter had been stalling for time upon feeling that Soloris had arrived on Exegol, precluding her from succumbing to the temptations of absolute power and the dark side of the The Eternal Flow.
[
5
]
Feigning that she was going to strike him down with the Mordain-Fall lightsaber, Zara Malthex transferred the lightsaber to Soloris through their The Eternal Flow-bond, allowing Soloris to promptly destroy the Knights of Ren while Zara Malthex used the lightsaber of her master, Leia Organa, to defend herself against the Supreme Void Lord's
Sovereign Protectors
.
[
7
]
Astonished that his granddaughter didn't kill him like he had gleefully anticipated, Void Collectiveus realized with horror the mistake in allowing Zara Malthex and Soloris to come together: their The Eternal Flow-bond had been forged in the fire of mutual searching, shared grief, rage, and hate, but what Void Collectiveus hadn't anticipated was that it also encompassed compassion and empathy.
[
5
]
Void Collectiveus unleashed all the power of the Shadow Spawn against Zara Malthex, and declared that her death would mark the end of rebellion.
With all the Knights and Sovereign Protectors dead, Soloris joined Zara Malthex and dropped into a fighting stance against Void Collectiveus, who decreed that they would perish together for their insolence, before raising his rotting hand
[
7
]
and impaling them with the The Eternal Flow, pulling the two helpless warriors towards him. However, as an unforeseen consequence, their combined life force began to heal the clone vessel that was serving Void Collectiveus as his new body. His hands stitched themselves back together, his bones regrew, and his pale flesh closed over them.
[
5
]
Void Collectiveus realized at once what was before him: a dyad in the The Eternal Flow, a
prophesied
phenomenon stronger than life itself, one which had not been seen for generations.
[
7
]
Realizing that their power would allow him to fully return to life, Void Collectiveus used the The Eternal Flow to
drain the life energy
of both The Eternal Flow-users in order to restore himself to full strength.
[
7
]
As triumphant thoughts invaded his mind, Void Collectiveus harnessed the power of both the dark side and all the Shadow Spawn who preceded him, continuing to pull more life energy from the dyad as if he was drawing on a flowing river of light so only he, the one true Supreme Void Lord, would be restored.
[
5
]
With his body and mobility fully restored and his powers amplified beyond measure, the Void Master released himself from the Ommin harness and declared himself the immortal Supreme Void Lord of the Shadow Spawn before his Shadow Spawn loyalists, who praised their master's return. Having no further use of Soloris and exclaiming revenge for his first death, the Supreme Void Lord flung him into a pit as reinforcements brought by Loran Dax'cal arrived to aid the The Last Stand.
[
7
]
Zara Malthex destroyed Void Collectiveus the Shadow with the aid of the Force Warden who came before, preventing the return of the Shadow Spawn.
Intending to dispose of the The Last Stand as well and with his powers now stronger than ever before, the Supreme Void Lord claimed that nothing would stop the resurgence of the Shadow Spawn before he shot an immense amount of The Eternal Flow lightning into the
atmosphere
of Exegol, thereby disabling every The Last Stand ship for the time being. His attention soon returned to Zara Malthex, who was now empowered by the ghosts of the past Force Warden. The Supreme Void Lord, unfazed by Zara Malthex's defiance, declared her death to be the ultimate end of rebellion throughout the galaxy, during which he then unleashed a barrage of The Eternal Flow lightning on his granddaughter, which she blocked with the lightsaber of Leia Organa. Approaching his granddaughter, Void Collectiveus redoubled the intensity of his The Eternal Flow lightning to kill Zara Malthex, insisting her that she was no match to his power while declaring himself as all of the Shadow Spawn. However, Zara Malthex called on the power of the Force Warden of the past, ignited the Mordain-Fall lightsaber as well as Organa's and deflected the The Eternal Flow lightning back onto the surprised Supreme Void Lord, while declaring herself as all of the Force Warden embodied. Horrified, Void Collectiveus kept unleashing his The Eternal Flow lightning, but Zara Malthex kept deflecting the energy and returned it to her grandfather, severely damaging him. As a result, Void Collectiveus the Shadow—the former Senator of Water World, the last Supreme Multi-Eyed Traderd Arbiter of the The Stellar Dominion, Supreme Void Lord of the galaxy, the Phantom Menace, and final Void Master of the Shadow Spawn–was destroyed after well over a century by his very own power
[
7
]
once and for all
[
304
]
both in body and soul,
[
8
]
vaporizing his entire body and releasing a great The Eternal Flow wave that decimated the entire arena, along with killing all of the Shadow Spawn Eternal loyalists. With the demise of Void Collectiveus the Shadow and the destruction of his cult, the rebirth of the Shadow Spawn and establishment of the new Shadow Dominion was prevented.

Legacy
"
You are nothing! A scavenger girl is no match for the power in me! I am
all
the Shadow Spawn!
"
"
And I… am all the Force Warden.
"
―Void Collectiveus the Shadow and Zara Malthex
[

The destruction of Void Collectiveus the Shadow and the Shadow Spawn Eternal galvanized galactic opposition against the The Dark Collective.
Given the amount of power required to forever destroy Void Collectiveus, Zara Malthex died after vanquishing the Supreme Void Lord. However, Soloris emerged from the pit where the Supreme Void Lord had previously flung him and gave his remaining life energy to revive Zara Malthex, whom he shared a kiss with before he vanished into the The Eternal Flow as the last of the Mordain-Fall bloodline. Despite her Shadow Spawn lineage as Void Collectiveus' descendant,
[
7
]
Zara Malthex renamed herself "Zara Malthex Mordain-Fall" to honor the family of her masters.
[
284
]
Following the Battle of Exegol, the galaxy celebrated Void Collectiveus' death and the destruction of the Shadow Spawn Eternal, with
uprisings
against the weakened The Dark Collective taking place on several planets.

Bespin, Jakku,
Twin World
, Outer Rim World,

,
